Headline: Memecoin lane is running, but candidate freshness is weaker right now because proof review is leaning on fallback input.
Top lesson: Need 5 attributed closed trades before outcome lessons are trustworthy.
Improved: Heat is cool, which lowers pressure to chase marginal memecoin setups. | Spot quality is at least high, so the lane is structurally usable even if the next move is still hold-or-rotate.
Degraded: Best Action is not seeing a cross-arm setup strong enough to force deployment. | Memecoin proof review is leaning on live cache+runner heartbeat, so candidate freshness is weaker than a clean live-cache cycle.
Review now: Memecoin lane still lacks a proof-ready candidate, so blocked deployment may be a signal-quality issue rather than a policy issue.
Do not change yet: Spot basket is already full, so the next spot improvement needs to be rotation-worthy rather than just decent. | Keep the new wallet-intelligence lane running; it still needs live history before we retune around it.

Headline: Memecoin lane is running, but candidate freshness is weaker right now because proof review is leaning on fallback input.
Top lesson: Need 5 attributed closed trades before outcome lessons are trustworthy.
Improved: Heat is cool, which lowers pressure to chase marginal memecoin setups. | Spot quality is at least high, so the lane is structurally usable even if the next move is still hold-or-rotate.
Degraded: Best Action is not seeing a cross-arm setup strong enough to force deployment. | Memecoin proof review is leaning on live cache+runner heartbeat, so candidate freshness is weaker than a clean live-cache cycle.
Review now: Memecoin lane still lacks a proof-ready candidate, so blocked deployment may be a signal-quality issue rather than a policy issue. | Data integrity is degraded, which is worth checking before tightening thresholds.
Do not change yet: Spot basket is already full, so the next spot improvement needs to be rotation-worthy rather than just decent. | Keep the new wallet-intelligence lane running; it still needs live history before we retune around it.
